## tech_space_exploration

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_exploration&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Space Exploration

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:41`

## tech_space_exploration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_exploration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超光速航行的实现带来了太空时代的曙光。跨星系载人航行现在不再仅仅是可能，而是变成一种必要的技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

The realization of faster-than-light travel is a new dawn for space exploration. Manned missions to other star systems are now not only possible, but necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:42`

## tech_corvettes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_corvettes&lang=en)

### 中文

护卫舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

Corvettes

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:43`

## tech_corvettes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_corvettes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超光速航行技术的突破将使得我们将传统的地空军用穿梭机最终改造成体型相对小，速度快，操纵性强但是火力有限的机动战舰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

The FTL breakthrough allowed early attempts to adapt traditional surface-to-space shuttle frames to military applications to finally create relatively small, fast, and maneuverable vessels with limited firepower.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:44`

## tech_destroyers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_destroyers&lang=en)

### 中文

驱逐舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Destroyers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:45`

## tech_destroyers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_destroyers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管比护卫舰大一些，但是驱逐舰的船体结构仍使它仍不失为一种较为灵活的炮艇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

While larger than Corvettes, Destroyer hull configurations still make for comparatively nimble gunboats.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:46`

## tech_cruisers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cruisers&lang=en)

### 中文

巡洋舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:43`

### English

Cruisers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:47`

## tech_cruisers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cruisers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种船体结构最初设计为下一代试验型殖民船，其丰富的挂载点使得其能够理想地携带重武器及弹药。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:44`

### English

Originally devised as an experimental next-generation colony ship hull configuration, the design's ample hardpoints made it ideal for carrying heavy weapons ordnance.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:48`

## tech_battleships

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_battleships&lang=en)

### 中文

战列舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Battleships

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:49`

## tech_battleships_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_battleships_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为最大的标准化军用战舰，战列舰自然配备了厚重的装甲和数个移动炮塔。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

The largest of the standard military vessels, Battleships are traditionally heavily armored and sport several gun batteries.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:50`

## tech_titans

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_titans&lang=en)

### 中文

泰坦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

Titans

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:51`

## tech_titans_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_titans_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

设计这种规模的战舰，可以结合卓越的火力和尖端的辅助，达到摧枯拉朽的效果。泰坦型战舰可充当全能的旗舰，也可支援周围的舰船，抑或诱敌深入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Designing ships at this scale allows for a deadly combination of exceptional firepower and cutting-edge auxiliary support. Titan configurations may serve as highly versatile flagships which are also capable of bolstering surrounding vessels or ensnaring hostiles.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:52`

## tech_colossus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colossus&lang=en)

### 中文

巨像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

Colossi

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:53`

## tech_colossus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colossus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨构工程学的进步孕育出了巨像型战舰，模糊了现实与幻想的界限。只需按下按钮，这些巨大的单一武器平台就能凭一己之力改变或抹去一整个世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

Advances in mega-engineering blur the lines between reality and fantasy in the form of Colossus-type military vessels. These immense, single-weapon platforms are capable of radically altering or obliterating entire worlds at the push of a button.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:54`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_corvette_hull_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最新一代的护卫舰优化了结构的整体性并改进了舱壁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

建立新的护卫舰模板和建造标准将会大大提高生产线的效率。

## tech_starbase_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_1&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星基地建设

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

Starbase Construction

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:110`

## tech_starbase_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了充分利用恒星系中的资源，我们必须在星系主星较近的轨道附近建立一个集中化的太空设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

A centralized space installation built in close orbit around the system primary is necessary to make full use of the resources in a star system.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:111`

## tech_starbase_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_2&lang=en)

### 中文

星港

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Starport

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:112`

## tech_starbase_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

初期的恒星基地只不过是临时的前哨站。而永久性的星港能够让我们保证对恒星系的牢固控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

The earliest starbases were little more than makeshift outposts. Permanent starports will enable us to maintain a firmer grip on star systems.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:113`

## tech_starbase_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_3&lang=en)

### 中文

星垒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Starhold

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:114`

## tech_starbase_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着太空交通流量的增加，我们需要更大更可靠的恒星基地来保证对恒星系航道的控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

As space traffic increases, larger and more defensible starbases are needed to maintain control over system spacelanes.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:115`

## tech_starbase_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_4&lang=en)

### 中文

星堡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

Star Fortress

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:116`

## tech_starbase_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些强大的太空堡垒将为恒星系中所有的军事活动提供一个据点，它们的存在几乎足以阻挡住最顽强的进攻者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

These mighty space forts will provide a focal point for all military activity in a star system, and their presence is sure to deter all but the most stubborn attackers.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:117`

## tech_starbase_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_5&lang=en)

### 中文

擎天堡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Citadel

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:118`

## tech_starbase_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starbase_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个用一座巨型军用空间站就能抵挡数支攻击舰队的构想正在制定当中。一旦实现，这些擎天堡将会成为恒星系中心的伟大纪念碑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Plans for an immense battlestation capable of holding its own against several strike fleets are now being drawn up. If realized, these citadels will become monuments to greatness as much as system hubs.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:119`

## tech_modular_engineering

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_modular_engineering&lang=en)

### 中文

模块化工程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Modular Engineering

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:121`

## tech_modular_engineering_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_modular_engineering_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

优化恒星基地的建造流程，使用模块化设计，这将可以减少目前建造的花费和时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Streamlined starbase construction procedures, using modular designs, will reduce the present build cost and time.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:122`

## tech_defense_platform_hull_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_defense_platform_hull_1&lang=en)

### 中文

改善结构完整性

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

Improved Structural Integrity

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:124`

## tech_defense_platform_hull_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_defense_platform_hull_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

强化过的构架与增强了冲击吸收能力的防爆护盾即使在巨大的压力下，也可以确保平台的结构完整性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

A reinforced framework and blast shields with enhanced impact absorption will ensure the structural integrity of the platform even under immense pressure.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:125`

## tech_solar_panel_network

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_solar_panel_network&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道能源转化技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

Orbital Energy Conversion

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:133`

## tech_solar_panel_network_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_solar_panel_network_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先进的太阳能板科技可以抵消恒星基地的运行成本。不过，这些太阳能板需要占用一个恒星基地的模块才能生产足够规模的能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Advances in solar panel technology could offset the operating costs of our starbases. The panels would require an entire starbase module to generate enough energy, however.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:134`

## tech_mining_terminal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_terminal&lang=en)

### 中文

矿业公会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Mining Guilds

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:135`

## tech_mining_terminal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_terminal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有组织的矿业工会能有效地监督恒星系内的资源开采。它可以使那些独立“盲目钻探”的矿工们得到控制，从而带来效率的提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

Organized mining guilds that supervise all resource extraction in a system. This leads to increased efficiency, as independent "wildcat" miners are brought to heel.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:136`

## tech_zero_gravity_assembly_sequence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_zero_gravity_assembly_sequence&lang=en)

### 中文

零重力框架

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Zero-G Work Frames

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:137`

## tech_zero_gravity_assembly_sequence_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_zero_gravity_assembly_sequence_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些框架配备了许多有用的工具，因此，工人在轨道工地间作业时，将更加安全快速。舱外活动任务从未如此简单。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

These frames come equipped with a multitude of useful tools, enabling workers to quickly and safely navigate orbital construction sites. E.V.A. missions in space have never been easier.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:138`

## tech_strike_craft_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_1&lang=en)

### 中文

航母作战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Carrier Operations

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:141`

## tech_strike_craft_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

短程载人舰载机，速度快，体型小，突防生存能力强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

These manned strike craft are launched from a mothership and rely on their speed and small size to survive long enough to punch through enemy defenses.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:142`

## tech_strike_craft_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_2&lang=en)

### 中文

改良型舰载机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Improved Strike Craft

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:143`

## tech_strike_craft_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新一代舰载机装备了改进的航空电子设备，同时拥有更优良的操作性能及更强劲的武器装备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The next generation of strike craft, featuring improved avionics, better handling characteristics and more powerful armaments.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:144`

## tech_strike_craft_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_3&lang=en)

### 中文

先进型舰载机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

Advanced Strike Craft

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:145`

## tech_strike_craft_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_strike_craft_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

深度改良型舰载机，性能远胜于先期版本，是每位飞行员梦寐以求的机型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

A further refinement in strike craft design, these advanced strike craft are a pilot's dream. Their performance far exceeds that of previous models.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:146`

## tech_planetary_defenses

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_defenses&lang=en)

### 中文

行星防御

### English

Planetary Defenses

## tech_planetary_defenses_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_defenses_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙浩瀚冰冷，觊觎谋划者狡诈无情，保护我们的家园，时刻准备着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

There may come a time when intellects, vast and cool and unsympathetic, regard our worlds with envy and draw plans against us. We must be ready.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:150`

## tech_assault_armies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_assault_armies&lang=en)

### 中文

星际远征军

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

Interplanetary Expeditionary Forces

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:151`

## tech_assault_armies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_assault_armies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

入侵星球绝非易事。军队出征前，须先克服重大后勤补给问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

The invasion of another world is a staggering enterprise. There are immense logistical challenges that must be overcome before we can muster our first interplanetary armies.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:152`

## tech_drop_pod

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_drop_pod&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道空降舱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Orbital Drop Pods

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:153`

## tech_drop_pod_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_drop_pod_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一次性轨道空降舱可替代脆弱的登陆艇，我军可借此快速投送轻装部队，无视地形条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Single-use orbital drop pods will lessen our reliance on vulnerable landing craft and allow us to quickly land light forces anywhere on a planet's surface.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:154`

## tech_ship_armor_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_1&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米复合材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Nanocomposite Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:157`

## tech_ship_armor_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

加强过的纳米装甲能够提供多一层的防护，有没有这一层防护，在战斗中可能就是生与死的区别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Reinforced nanocomposite hull plating can provide an extra layer of protection that could be the difference between life and death in a combat situation.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:158`

## tech_ship_armor_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_2&lang=en)

### 中文

瓷金复合材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Ceramo-Metal Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:159`

## tech_ship_armor_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不同金属和陶瓷的结合物，坚硬而又不失柔韧性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

A combination of different metals and ceramics that result in a strong armor without sacrificing flexibility.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:160`

## tech_ship_armor_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_3&lang=en)

### 中文

塑钢材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Plasteel Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:161`

## tech_ship_armor_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

塑钢由多种塑料和金属结合而成，富有弹性。能增加舰体强度，而且造价低廉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Plasteel is an elastic material that combines many of the qualities of plastics and metal. It is relatively cheap to produce and can be used to augment ship armor.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:162`

## tech_ship_armor_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_4&lang=en)

### 中文

耐钢材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

Durasteel Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:163`

## tech_ship_armor_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此种材料质地强韧、耐冲击，是合适的舰体材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

This strong yet flexible material is capable of withstanding severe punishment. It serves well as ship armor.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:164`

## tech_ship_armor_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_5&lang=en)

### 中文

零素材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

Neutronium Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:165`

## tech_ship_armor_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ship_armor_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

中子装甲密度大、质地坚，相比常规合金装甲，中子装甲能抵抗更强大火力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

This incredibly dense and strong material can resist firepower that would turn armor made of any other alloys into slag.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:166`

## tech_crystal_armor_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_crystal_armor_1&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶复合镀层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

Crystal-Infused Plating

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:168`

## tech_crystal_armor_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_crystal_armor_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灌注水晶的镀层可使船体更加耐用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

By infusing armor plating with Crystalline matter we are able to further increase the durability of our ships' protective hulls.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:169`

## tech_crystal_armor_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_crystal_armor_2&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶锻造镀层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Crystal-Forged Plating

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:170`

## tech_crystal_armor_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_crystal_armor_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

锻造时加入水晶材料，则合金硬度更上一层楼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Introducing Crystalline matter into the forging process further improves the ultra-hard qualities of crystal-infused metals.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:171`

## tech_dragon_armor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dragon_armor&lang=en)

### 中文

人造龙鳞装甲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

Artificial Dragonscales

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:173`

## tech_dragon_armor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dragon_armor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

以太龙麟由多层独特矿物质纳米结构构成，具自我修复能力。采用非常规制造手段能够让我们精确仿制出这些能够自我修复的结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

The Ether Drake's scales are made up of multiple layers of unique mineral nanostructures. Unorthodox manufacturing techniques allow us to closely emulate these self-repairing structures.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:174`

## tech_thrusters_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_1&lang=en)

### 中文

化学推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Chemical Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:182`

## tech_thrusters_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

简易而有效的化学能推进器，依靠可燃推进剂来维持运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Simple yet moderately effective chemical thrusters that rely on combustible propellant to function.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:183`

## tech_thrusters_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_2&lang=en)

### 中文

离子推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Ion Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:184`

## tech_thrusters_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此种电推进器的推力来源于离子束，而非传统工质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

These electric thrusters use beams of ions to generate thrust without the need for propellant.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:185`

## tech_thrusters_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_3&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Plasma Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:186`

## tech_thrusters_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子推进是电推进的高级形式，赋予舰船更快的亚光速航速和更好的机动性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

An advanced form of electric propulsion, plasma thrusters afford ships greater sublight speeds and maneuverability.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:187`

## tech_thrusters_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_4&lang=en)

### 中文

脉冲推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Impulse Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:188`

## tech_thrusters_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_thrusters_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在亚光速条件下，此种高级聚变推进器可赋予舰船无与伦比的机动性和速度优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

These highly advanced fusion-powered thrusters provide ships with unmatched maneuverability and speed under sublight conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:189`

## tech_afterburners_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_afterburners_1&lang=en)

### 中文

加力燃烧室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Afterburners

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:191`

## tech_afterburners_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_afterburners_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种强力推进器能够额外提升战斗速度，然而需要消耗巨大能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

These powerful thrusters provide additional combat speed but consume a great deal of power.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:192`

## tech_afterburners_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_afterburners_2&lang=en)

### 中文

先进加力燃烧室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Advanced Afterburners

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:193`

## tech_afterburners_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_afterburners_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种改进版的标准加力燃烧室能够极大提升舰船的战斗速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

This improved version of the standard afterburners provide an even greater boost to a ship's combat speed.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:194`

## tech_space_defense_station_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_1&lang=en)

### 中文

深空防御

### English

Deep Space Defenses

## tech_space_defense_station_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这将允许我们建设深空防御平台以保护我们的恒星基地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

This will enable the construction of defensive deep space platforms to protect our Starbases.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:198`

Deep Space Stations

## tech_space_defense_station_2_desc

### 中文

藉由新的施工方式，我们能够在深空中建立更大型的防御站点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

New construction methods will allow for the construction of even larger military stations in the depths of space.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:200`

## tech_space_defense_station_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_3&lang=en)

深空据点

Deep Space Installations

## tech_space_defense_station_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些大型的据点如同太空中漂浮的城市。数以千计的军人驻扎其中，他们的装备火力堪比一整只舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

These massive installations are like cities floating in space. They can support a crew of thousands, and are often equipped with firepower rivaling that of a fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:202`

## tech_space_defense_station_improvement

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_improvement&lang=en)

工兵部队

### English

Corps of Engineers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:203`

## tech_space_defense_station_improvement_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_defense_station_improvement_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

创建专门的舰队工兵部队会有利于深空防御平台建设，同时降低建筑成本，并提高其耐久度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

The creation of a dedicated naval engineering corps will greatly benefit the construction of deep space defense platforms, decreasing building costs and increasing durability.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:204`

## tech_space_defense_station_improvement_machine_intelligence

空间站建造优化

### English

Optimized Station Construction

## tech_space_defense_station_improvement_desc_machine_intelligence

深入测评现有防御站的建筑方法，发掘潜在的改良之处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

An extensive evaluation of present military station construction methods has identified several areas of potential improvement.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:206`

## tech_housing_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_1&lang=en)

### 中文

气候控制系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

Weather Control Systems

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:209`

## tech_housing_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改变行星气候规律以压制灾难性气候现象的能力可使住房建筑更容易维护，并开放新的可居住区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

The ability to alter a planet's weather patterns to suppress destructive weather phenomena will make residential buildings easier to maintain and open up new areas for habitation.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:210`

## tech_housing_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_2&lang=en)

### 中文

反重力工程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Anti-Gravity Engineering

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:211`

## tech_housing_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在指向性重力科技的帮助下，星球地表的建筑可以达到前所未有的高度，而不必担心结构崩塌。通天塔指日可待！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

With directional gravity technology, buildings on the surface of planets can reach higher than ever before without risking structural collapse. The sky is the limit!

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:212`

## tech_housing_agrarian_idyll

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_agrarian_idyll&lang=en)

### 中文

农业乌托邦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Agrarian Utopias

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:213`

## tech_housing_agrarian_idyll_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_housing_agrarian_idyll_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过建立受气候制约的乡村公社，我们能够在不破坏农耕生活方式的情况下供养更多人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Through the establishment of weather-controlled rural communes, we can support a larger population without disrupting our agrarian way of life.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:214`

## tech_mechanized_mining

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mechanized_mining&lang=en)

机械化开采

### English

Mechanized Mining

## tech_mechanized_mining_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mechanized_mining_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

应用机械工程学进行采矿是任何工业社会的基本需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

The application of mechanical engineering to mineral extraction is a fundamental requirement for any industrial society.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:217`

Industrial Base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_basic_industry_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建立基础工业是远航宇宙的必要条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

The basic industrial infrastructure that is required to become a spacefaring civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:219`

## tech_powered_exoskeletons

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_powered_exoskeletons&lang=en)

动力外骨骼

### English

Powered Exoskeletons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:220`

## tech_powered_exoskeletons_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_powered_exoskeletons_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

外骨骼动力套装能增加穿戴者的力量和速度。其军事用途显而易见，但同样可以用来提升劳动效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

Wearing a powered exoskeleton suit augments the user's strength and speed. The military applications are obvious, but it will also increase labor efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:221`

## tech_powered_exoskeletons_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_powered_exoskeletons_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

改良电动机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Improved Servomotors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:222`

## tech_powered_exoskeletons_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_powered_exoskeletons_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

动力更强的电动机能增加机器人单位的载重能力。开采单位可运送更多待处理矿物，战争机器也能装载更为重型的武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

More powerful servomotors will increase the weight our robotic units can carry. Mining units can haul more minerals off to be processed, and war machines can be fitted with heavier armaments.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:223`

## tech_mining_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_1&lang=en)

### 中文

地热水力压裂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Geothermal Fracking

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:224`

## tech_mining_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新的水力压裂技术可使我们深入地壳，开采过去无法获得的地热资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

New fracking techniques will allow us to tap into previously unattainable pockets of geothermal resources deep beneath the planet's crust.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:225`

## tech_mining_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_2&lang=en)

### 中文

地心开采

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Deep Core Mining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:226`

## tech_mining_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

借助这些大型钻机，我们可以开采接近地心附近常见的深层富矿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

These massive drills are needed to access the rich mineral deposits that can often be found close to a planet's core.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:227`

## tech_mining_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_3&lang=en)

### 中文

矿物分离

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Mineral Isolation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:228`

## tech_mining_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

用先进光谱测定法配合水力压裂技术与化学冲刷，可以快速开采浅层与中层的矿脉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Advanced spectrometry combines with fracking techniques and chemical flushing to rapidly exploit shallow and mid-depth deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:229`

## tech_synchronized_work_patterns

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synchronized_work_patterns&lang=en)

### 中文

同步工作模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Synchronized Work Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:232`

## tech_synchronized_work_patterns_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synchronized_work_patterns_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

同步采矿网络的工作模式，进一步增加生产效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

By synchronizing the work patterns of our mining networks, their output efficiency can be further increased.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:233`

## tech_engineering_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_1&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米力学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Nanomechanics

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:234`

## tech_engineering_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

机械工程的进一步突破需要我们下到纳米尺度。现在我们能够以前所未有的方式操纵物理系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Further breakthroughs in mechanical engineering required us to descend to the nanometric scale. We are now able to manipulate physical systems in never-before-seen ways.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:235`

## tech_engineering_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_2&lang=en)

### 中文

自连接电路

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Self-Connecting Circuitry

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:236`

## tech_engineering_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像有机神经元通过轴突发芽重建失去的连接一样，这些回路也有自我修复的能力。如今，再简单的计算系统也有望在使用过程中不断发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Much as organic neurons reforge lost connections by means of axonal sprouting, these circuits are capable of self-overhaul. Even the simplest computing systems are now expected to evolve while in use.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:237`

## tech_engineering_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_3&lang=en)

### 中文

超固体材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Supersolid Materials

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:238`

## tech_engineering_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engineering_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些材料的量子力学特性是叠加态的关键，这是材料科学和工业技术的革命性发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

The quantum mechanical properties of these materials hold the key to superposition states, a revolutionary development for both material and industrial science.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:239`

## tech_robotic_workers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_robotic_workers&lang=en)

### 中文

人造劳动力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

Artificial Workforce

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:242`

## tech_robotic_workers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_robotic_workers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此种人造劳力单元是完美的廉价低端劳动力，能在简单的语音指令下完成重体力劳动，但无法胜任诸如爬楼梯等较为高级的任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

These artificial work units are perfect for menial labor tasks. They are hard-working and capable of following simple vocal instructions, but should not be expected to climb stairs.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:243`

## tech_droid_workers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_droid_workers&lang=en)

### 中文

人造专家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Artificial Specialists

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:244`

## tech_droid_workers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_droid_workers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级版人造单元，行动能力可与大多数有机生物相媲美。其神经处理器也进步巨大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Upgraded artificial units have an expanded movement range equal to that of most organics. Their neural processors have also been greatly improved.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:245`

## tech_synthetic_workers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synthetic_workers&lang=en)

### 中文

人造管理层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Artificial Administration

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:246`

## tech_synthetic_workers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synthetic_workers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高级单元，比绝大多数有机生物更强，更快，更耐用。其神经处理器有很大提高，拥有独立运作的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Highly advanced units that are stronger, faster and more durable than the vast majority of organics. With their upgraded neural processors they are fully capable of independent operations.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:247`

## tech_synthetic_leaders

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synthetic_leaders&lang=en)

### 中文

人造人格矩阵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Artificial Personality Matrix

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:250`

## tech_synthetic_leaders_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synthetic_leaders_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

合成人被赋予人格后，终于能与其他人们平等地进行社会交流。有些合成人甚至能够成为领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

Equipped with a personality, synthetics will finally be able to interact socially on equal terms with the rest of our citizens. Some units may even assume leadership roles.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:251`

## tech_integrated_cybernetics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_integrated_cybernetics&lang=en)

整合式义体

### English

Integrated Cybernetics

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:252`

## tech_integrated_cybernetics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_integrated_cybernetics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对基础的外骨骼套件进行改良后，这些整合式义体植入体能够让使用者对于有害的环境效果更具有抵抗力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Improving upon basic exoskeletal suits, these integrated cybernetic implants make the user more resistant to harmful environmental effects.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:253`

## tech_integrated_cybernetics_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_integrated_cybernetics_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

自适应义体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

### 中文

在我们能够在其他星球上建立轨道空间站之前，工程师们必须首先解决空间建设中遇到的全新的设计挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Construction in space presents our engineers with new design challenges that must be solved before we can build orbital stations above other worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:286`

## tech_mining_station_improvement_1

On-Site Processing

## tech_mining_station_improvement_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_station_improvement_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

直接在星球矿物开采处的地表进行矿物加工，能够显著提高采矿效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

By processing minerals directly above the world where they are extracted, our mining efficiency is substantially improved.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:292`

## tech_mining_station_improvement_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_station_improvement_2&lang=en)

自动采矿作业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Autonomous Mining Operations

## tech_mining_station_improvement_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_station_improvement_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

依靠自动化机械的广泛应用，一小队骨干人员即可管理一个开采站。此举能够降低成本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

By relying extensively on automated drones, mining stations can be operated with only a small skeleton crew. This will lower their costs.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:294`

## tech_mine_satramene

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_satramene&lang=en)

### 中文

萨特拉烯精炼

### English

Satramene Refining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:295`

## tech_mine_satramene_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_satramene_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种于某些星球上天然产出的刺激性气体。可代替镇静剂，对绝大多数生物都有镇静效果，也可用来当做非杀伤性的制敌手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

This gas is a naturally occurring stimulant found on some worlds. It has a calming effect on most beings, and can be used as a non-violent way of pacifying hostile individuals.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:296`

## tech_mine_garanthium

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_garanthium&lang=en)

### 中文

珈蓝矿开采

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Garanthium Mining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:297`

## tech_mine_garanthium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_garanthium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成功建立珈蓝矿场是一项独特的考验，因为其可延展性，它的开采难度同其吸引力一样巨大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Establishing successful Garanthium quarries poses unique challenges, as the malleable qualities of the ore make it as difficult to mine as it is attractive.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:298`

## tech_mine_orillium

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_orillium&lang=en)

### 中文

奥利姆矿开采

### English

Orillium Mining

## tech_mine_orillium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_orillium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

稳定粒子射线能为易爆的奥利姆矿开采提供相对安全的环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Stabilization beams allow relatively safe extraction of volatile Orillium ores.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:300`

## tech_mine_living_metal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_living_metal&lang=en)

活体金属

### English

Living Metal

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:301`

## tech_mine_living_metal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_living_metal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

开采和处理罕见的活体金属极其困难，但如果成功使用这些易变金属，获得的收益也无法估量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Mining and processing the rare living metals is exceedingly difficult, but successful application of the mutable alloys yields incomparable advantages.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:302`

## tech_mine_betharian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_betharian&lang=en)

### 中文

贝赛里安精炼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Betharian Refining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:303`

## tech_mine_betharian_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_betharian_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

贝塞里安矿最早似乎出自单一矿源，但千年前，开始出现于星系各处。此类矿物成鹅卵石大小，群状分布，并且高度易燃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

The Betharian minerals appear to have originated from singular source, but were scattered across the galaxy millennia ago. The minerals are most often encountered as pebble-sized clusters, and are highly flammable.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:304`

## tech_mine_rare_crystals

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_rare_crystals&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Rare Crystal Mining

## tech_mine_rare_crystals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_rare_crystals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在不损坏甚至是摧毁它们的前提下开采这些水晶，是一件对精密性要求很高的工作，需要极度精密的先进设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Mining these crystals without damaging or even destroying them is an extremely delicate process. It requires advanced equipment capable of pinpoint precision.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:306`

为了完成此等规模的建造项目，我们必须首先对与巨型结构相关的工程学和物资储备需求有基本的了解。

## tech_mass_drivers_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

线圈炮是早期质量投射器的升级版，使用电磁线圈更有效地加速弹药。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

Coilguns are more powerful versions of the early mass drivers, using electromagnetic coils to rapidly accelerate projectiles towards targets.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:326`

## tech_mass_drivers_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_3&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Railguns

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:327`

## tech_mass_drivers_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道炮是最新的电磁加速炮，轨道炮通过并行双轨加速炮弹使之以难以置信的高速发射出去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

The latest in electromagnetic accelerators, railguns use twin parallel rails to fire projectiles at incredible speeds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:328`

## tech_mass_drivers_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_4&lang=en)

### 中文

先进轨道炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Advanced Railguns

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:329`

## tech_mass_drivers_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经典轨道炮的新版本，使用更坚固的轨道并增加了能量配给。这些武器比之前的更强劲，更准确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

A new version of the venerable railgun, with sturdier rails and an enhanced power supply. These weapons are even more powerful and accurate than their predecessors.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:330`

## tech_mass_drivers_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_5&lang=en)

### 中文

高斯炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Gauss Cannons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:331`

## tech_mass_drivers_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_drivers_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对老式电磁线圈的设计进行升级后，新版本的破坏性甚至高于最先进的轨道炮。它能够发射得更准，速度更快。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

This updated version of the old coilgun design is even more destructive than the latest railgun designs. It is capable of launching projectiles with incredible accuracy and speed.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:332`

## tech_kinetic_artillery_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_kinetic_artillery_1&lang=en)

动能火炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Kinetic Battery

## tech_kinetic_artillery_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_kinetic_artillery_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

射程极远的火炮，火力猛烈，而且准确性极高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Very long-range artillery capable of hurling powerful rounds over extreme distances with a large degree of accuracy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:334`

## tech_kinetic_artillery_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_kinetic_artillery_2&lang=en)

先进动能火炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Kinetic Artillery

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:335`

## tech_kinetic_artillery_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_kinetic_artillery_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

更为强劲的远程舰载火力系统，装备有升级版的发射装置和火控系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

An even more powerful long-range, ship-based artillery cannon, equipped with a new barrel and an upgraded fire control computer.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:336`

## tech_mass_accelerator_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_accelerator_1&lang=en)

### 中文

兆级加农炮

### English

Mega Cannon

## tech_mass_accelerator_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_accelerator_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一款无比庞大的质量加速炮，只能安装在轴基武器台上。能够在极远距离射出威力惊人的炮弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

An enormous mass accelerator cannon that can only be placed in a spinal mount and fires monstrously powerful rounds over vast distances.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:338`

## tech_mass_accelerator_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_accelerator_2&lang=en)

### 中文

千兆级加农炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Giga Cannon

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:339`

## tech_mass_accelerator_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mass_accelerator_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

更加强大的质量加速炮，能够轻易撕毁最坚固的护盾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

An even more powerful mass accelerator cannon that can rip through even the toughest shields.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:340`

## tech_autocannons_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_1&lang=en)

机关炮

### English

Autocannons

## tech_autocannons_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大口径的弹道武器，发抛射壳炮弹。这些机炮的主要优势在于极高的射速。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Large-caliber ballistic weapons that fire cased explosive shells. The main advantage of these cannons is their high rate of fire.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:344`

## tech_autocannons_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_2&lang=en)

### 中文

撕裂者机关炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Ripper Cannons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:345`

## tech_autocannons_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些升级版机关炮具备更完善的追踪能力和开火控制系统，因此也提升了射速。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

These upgraded rotary autocannons feature improved tracking and fire control systems. This allows them to maintain an even higher rate of fire.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:346`

## tech_autocannons_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_3&lang=en)

### 中文

火风暴机关炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

Stormfire Cannons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:347`

## tech_autocannons_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocannons_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

设计先进的自动火炮，能够向目标倾泻一片由高爆炮弹构成的弹幕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Advanced rotary autocannons capable of spewing a cloud of high-explosive shells at a target.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:348`

## tech_flak_batteries_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_1&lang=en)

### 中文

高射炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Flak Battery

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:350`

## tech_flak_batteries_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高射炮可以近距离发射榴弹，这对敌人的舰载机杀伤巨大，当然也能够防御导弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Gun batteries that fire shrapnel rounds at close range. They are particularly lethal to enemy strike craft, but can also defend against missiles.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:351`

## tech_flak_batteries_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_2&lang=en)

### 中文

高射加农炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Flak Cannons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:352`

## tech_flak_batteries_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级后的高射炮装备了新型更加强大的榴弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Upgraded flak batteries with new, more powerful shrapnel rounds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:353`

## tech_flak_batteries_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_3&lang=en)

### 中文

重型高射炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Flak Artillery

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:354`

## tech_flak_batteries_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_flak_batteries_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用预测跟踪算法和更强大的齐射，使得这些高射炮拥有了难以置信的打击敌军舰载机的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Predictive tracking algorithms and even more powerful rounds make these batteries incredibly lethal to enemy strike craft.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:355`

## tech_missiles_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_1&lang=en)

### 中文

核导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

Nuclear Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:358`

## tech_missiles_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

配备有高当量核弹头的天基导弹。尽管是前太空时代的老旧技术，这种简单粗暴的制导武器在射程范围内有着惊人的高效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Space-to-space missiles equipped with high-yield nuclear warheads. Although based on old pre-space technology, these crude fire-and-forget weapons can be surprisingly effective at range.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:359`

## tech_missiles_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_2&lang=en)

### 中文

聚变核导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

Fusion Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:360`

## tech_missiles_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级版的深空导弹，配备了更强大的核聚变弹头，飞行能力进一步提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

These updated space-to-space missiles have more powerful fusion warheads and improved flight performance.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:361`

## tech_missiles_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_3&lang=en)

### 中文

反物质导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

Antimatter Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:362`

## tech_missiles_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些先进的导弹配备有强劲的反物质弹头。更加完善的导航系统大大提高了索敌能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Advanced missiles equipped with very powerful antimatter warheads. A new and improved guidance system gives them a better chance of finding their targets.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:363`

## tech_missiles_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_4&lang=en)

### 中文

量子导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Quantum Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:364`

## tech_missiles_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最新的深空导弹，升级之后装备了威力极度强大的弹头，它能够从真空中汲取真空零点能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

The latest version of space-to-space missile, this upgraded variant has an immensely powerful warhead that draws its energy from a zero point vacuum.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:365`

## tech_missiles_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_5&lang=en)

### 中文

掠夺者导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Marauder Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:366`

## tech_missiles_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_missiles_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些最尖端的导弹配备有限程度的人工智能导航，极大地提高导弹追踪目标的能力和表现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

These highly advanced missiles are equipped with limited onboard AI pilots that substantially improve their performance and ability to track targets.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:367`

## tech_swarmer_missiles_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_swarmer_missiles_1&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂群导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

Swarmer Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:368`

## tech_swarmer_missiles_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_swarmer_missiles_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些小导弹可以集群发射，是专为克制敌人的点防御系统设计的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

These smaller missiles are launched in volleys and have been specifically designed to overwhelm enemy point-defense systems.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:369`

## tech_swarmer_missiles_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_swarmer_missiles_2&lang=en)

### 中文

旋风导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Whirlwind Missiles

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:370`

## tech_swarmer_missiles_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_swarmer_missiles_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新的微型化技术将使得我们可以在蜂群导弹上安装更加强力的弹头，并提高飞行能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

New miniaturization techniques will allow us to equip swarmer missiles with more powerful warheads and improved flight performance.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:371`

## tech_torpedoes_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_1&lang=en)

### 中文

太空鱼雷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Space Torpedoes

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:374`

## tech_torpedoes_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些鱼雷其实都是速度缓慢的巨大导弹。它们装备了更加强大的弹头和护盾调制器，这使得它们可以安然穿过能量屏障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

These torpedoes are essentially very large, slow-moving missiles. They are equipped with more powerful warheads and shield modulators that let them slip unmolested through energy screens.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:375`

## tech_torpedoes_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_2&lang=en)

### 中文

装甲鱼雷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Armored Torpedoes

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:376`

## tech_torpedoes_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级过后的太空鱼雷装备有装甲，可以更好地应对敌人的点防御系统。在爆炸的时候，鱼雷碎裂的装甲也会造成额外的伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Upgraded space torpedoes encased in armor, to better withstand enemy point-defense fire. The armor fragments into shards that cause additional damage when the torpedo detonates.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:377`

## tech_torpedoes_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_3&lang=en)

### 中文

毁灭者鱼雷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

Devastator Torpedoes

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:378`

## tech_torpedoes_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_torpedoes_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

装备有超强弹头的先进鱼雷。拥有更加光滑的设计，更快的引擎以及改良的护盾调解器，可以轻松穿过能量屏障。

### 中文

宇宙的基本力可以通过物理场来调节。掌握了这个过程，我们就可以扮演上帝的角色——至少在科学实验室的范围内是这样。

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:442`

注资行星际资源库，灵活审批研究计划。

### English

A bid for interplanetary resource pools and more flexible research grants.
