## tech_neuro_quantum_links_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_neuro_quantum_links_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先进的神经植入体连接着量子计算集群，让人能够进行并行思考。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Advanced neurological implants with connection to quantum computing clusters allow for multi-threaded thoughts.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:446`

## tech_cryostasis_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1&lang=en)

### 中文

自动化殖民船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Automated Colony Ships

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:447`

## tech_cryostasis_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将殖民船交由机载电脑，而船员和殖民者可以进入假死状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

By giving control of the colony ship to the on-board computer, the crew and colonists can be placed in suspended animation.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:448`

## tech_cryostasis_1_wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1_wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

幼苗孵化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Seedling Incubation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:449`

## tech_cryostasis_1_wilderness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1_wilderness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过在新殖民地的原生土壤中预先培育新幼苗，我们可以加快其适应过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

By first incubating our new seedlings in the native soils of new colonies we can speed up the acclimatization process.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:450`

## tech_cryostasis_1_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民地建设无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Colony Fabrication Drones

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:451`

## tech_cryostasis_1_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_1_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

在殖民船的货舱中储备数队小型建筑无人机。到达行星地表后启动，大幅促进初期殖民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

Fleets of small but efficient construction drones that are stored in the cargo bays of colony ships. Once activated on a planetary surface, they greatly speed up the initial settlement process.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:452`

## tech_cryostasis_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_2&lang=en)

### 中文

自我意识殖民船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

Self-Aware Colony Ships

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:453`

## tech_cryostasis_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

相当先进的系统，知悉目标后，能自动选择执行最优方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Sufficiently advanced systems are capable of optimizing themselves if they are made acutely aware of the purpose for which they were made.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:454`

## tech_cryostasis_2_wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_2_wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

永久孵化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Permanent Incubation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:455`

## tech_cryostasis_2_wilderness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryostasis_2_wilderness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

永久维持能够适应所有气候类型的特种苗木，可大幅缩短我们创建新世界所需的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Permanently maintaining specialized seedlings capable of surviving all types of climates massively decreases the time it takes us to create new worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:456`

## tech_automated_exploration

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_automated_exploration&lang=en)

### 中文

自动探索协议

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Automated Exploration Protocols

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:457`

## tech_automated_exploration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_automated_exploration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先进的人工智能可以辅助科学专员探索新的世界、寻找新的生命和新的文明、以及前往从未有任何布洛葛去过的地方。通过人工智能的帮助，科研船可以探索宇宙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

An advanced AI will aid science officers in their task to explore strange new worlds, to seek out new life and new civilizations, to boldly go where no Blorg has gone before. The Science Ship will be able to explore the galaxy with help from an AI.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:458`

## tech_automated_exploration_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_automated_exploration_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

自主探索算法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Autonomous Exploration Algorithms

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:459`

## tech_automated_exploration_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_automated_exploration_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

探索单位的算法更新换代后，勘察更快，并可在无中央机械智能指示时自主探索深空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Updated and more advanced algorithms will not only speed up surveying, but also permit our exploration assets to spend more time in deep space without continuous instructions from the central Machine Intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:460`

异星天然气可以人工生产，但是较为困难，需要先进的精炼设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Exotic Gas can be produced artificially, but it is a difficult process that requires advanced refinery equipment.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:466`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_volatile_motes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

除了明显的安全问题之外，人造易爆微粒本身就是极端精密且复杂的工作，需要高度专业化的设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Aside from the obvious safety concerns, the artificial creation of Volatile Motes is an extremely delicate and complicated process that requires highly specialized equipment.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:468`

### 中文

结晶化的过程复杂且困难，但只要有专门的生产工厂，我们就可以生产人工制造水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

The crystallization process is complicated and difficult, but synthetic crystals can be manufactured by using specialized industrial plants.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:470`

## tech_mine_neutronium

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_neutronium&lang=en)

零素提取

### English

Neutronium Extraction

## tech_mine_neutronium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_neutronium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超高密度中子实质可用于锻造高强度、高耐久装甲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Neutronium is a super-dense substance composed of neutrons that can be forged into extremely resistant and durable armor.

## tech_mine_dark_matter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_dark_matter&lang=en)

### English

Dark Matter Drawing

## tech_mine_dark_matter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mine_dark_matter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种独特的物质具有许多打破物理原理的特性。仅在黑洞周围或部分星云附近能获得。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

This exotic substance has many properties that seemingly defy the laws of physics. Harvestable concentrations can only be found near Black Holes or in certain nebulas.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:475`

## tech_fission_power

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_fission_power&lang=en)

### 中文

裂变能源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

Fission Power

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:478`

## tech_fission_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_fission_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

引发核裂变为舰船提供能源，但同时也会生成有害核废料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

This energy source generates power for ships through induced fission, but it also produces harmful nuclear waste.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:479`

## tech_fusion_power

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_fusion_power&lang=en)

### 中文

聚变能源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Fusion Power

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:480`

## tech_fusion_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_fusion_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

核聚变可为舰船提供大量能源，且风险小于裂变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Nuclear fusion processes generate a great amount of power for ships, but without many of the risks associated with fission power.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:481`

## tech_cold_fusion_power

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cold_fusion_power&lang=en)

### 中文

冷核聚变能源

### English

Cold Fusion Power

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:482`

## tech_cold_fusion_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cold_fusion_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

低温核聚变技术将促进新一代舰载聚变反应堆诞生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

The ability to sustain a fusion reaction at relatively low temperatures will result in a new generation of fusion reactors for our ships.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:483`

## tech_antimatter_power

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_antimatter_power&lang=en)

### 中文

反物质能源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Antimatter Power

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:484`

## tech_antimatter_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_antimatter_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用正反物质结合产生舰船能源，效率远超核聚变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

By harnessing the energy produced by matter/antimatter annihilations, our efficiency at generating ship power will be orders of magnitude better than fusion.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:485`

## tech_zero_point_power

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_zero_point_power&lang=en)

### 中文

零点能源

### English

Zero Point Power

## tech_zero_point_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_zero_point_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

提取真空零点能是目前最高效的产能方式，可以近乎无限地供应舰船能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

These generators extract vacuum energy, providing an almost limitless supply of ship power. There is no more efficient way to generate energy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:487`

## tech_dark_matter_power_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dark_matter_power_core&lang=en)

Dark Matter Power

### 中文

以某种违背物理法则的方法，直接从暗物质中汲取巨量能源，为舰船供能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

### 中文

能量偏射屏障可在过载前化解一定敌方火力，主要缺点是能耗高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Energy deflector screens can stop a limited amount of incoming enemy fire before overloading. Their main drawback is that they consume large amounts of power.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_shields_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新型护盾生成器是新一代能量屏障，取代旧式能量偏转器，提供更优秀的防护能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

## tech_sensors_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_2&lang=en)

### 中文

重力传感器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Gravitic Sensors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:540`

## tech_sensors_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些传感器已经过调整，可以检测太空中的任何引力变化，例如由舰船出现导致的力场扭曲波动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

These sensors have been tuned to look for any gravitational changes in space, such as those produced by the presence of a starship.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:541`

## tech_sensors_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_3&lang=en)

### 中文

亚空间传感器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Subspace Sensors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:542`

## tech_sensors_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些传感器能检测到旅行舰船启动后遗留的亚空间波动。它们的灵敏度和范围比重力传感器阵列更大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Sensors that detect the subspace fluctuations left in the wake of traveling starships. Their sensitivity and range is greater than that of a gravitic sensor array.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:543`

## tech_sensors_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_4&lang=en)

### 中文

快子传感器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Tachyon Sensors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:544`

## tech_sensors_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_sensors_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先进的传感器依靠旋转的快子束，即使在极远距离也能检测船舶运动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Advanced sensors that rely on rotating tachyon beams to detect ship movements even at extreme distances.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:545`

## tech_orbital_sensor_array

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_orbital_sensor_array&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道传感器阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

Orbital Sensor Arrays

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:546`

## tech_orbital_sensor_array_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_orbital_sensor_array_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可添加到轨道太空港结构中的强力传感器阵列。它们的传感器将周边区域完美覆盖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Powerful sensor arrays that can be added to the structure of orbital spaceports. They provide excellent sensor coverage of the surrounding region of space.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:547`

## tech_power_plant_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_plant_1&lang=en)

### English

## tech_power_plant_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_plant_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可以储存巨量能源的电容器是现代文明的基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Capacitors capable of storing vast amounts of energy is one of the cornerstones for any modern civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:551`

## tech_power_plant_2

### 中文

### English

Field Modulation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:552`

调整过的力场可以增加能源产量并提高能源输出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Modulated fields allow for increased production and improved energy yield.

Basic entanglement principles applied to energy generation provide a substantial increase in energy output.

处于量子叠加态下的能量线圈，可同时充放电。

Quantum superposition of energy coils, simultaneously drained and charged.

## tech_stellar_absorption

恒星吸收

Stellar Absorption

## tech_power_hub_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_hub_1&lang=en)

### 中文

全球能源管理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Global Energy Management

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:562`

## tech_power_hub_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_hub_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

负载平衡设施可以大幅提升本地电网的稳定性，并将多余的能源返还中央电容器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Load-balancing facilities can greatly improve the stability of local grids and feed excess power back into centralized capacitors.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:563`

## tech_power_hub_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_hub_2&lang=en)

### 中文

行星电力网

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Planetary Power Grid

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:564`

## tech_power_hub_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_power_hub_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

重新设计后统一的电网将会进一步减少远距离传输的能源损耗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

A re-engineered and unified grid further reduces energy losses over distance.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:565`

## tech_wormhole_generation_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_1&lang=en)

### 中文

亚空间虫洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Subspace Wormholes

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:568`

## tech_wormhole_generation_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在亚空间中打通连接，可以在正常空间中两个不同点之间建立一条临时通道。点与点之间的穿梭将是几乎瞬时的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

By tunneling directly through subspace, a temporary conduit can be established between two different points in normal space. Travel between these points would be near instantaneous.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:569`

## tech_wormhole_generation_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_2&lang=en)

### 中文

虫洞校准矩阵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Wormhole Calibration Matrix

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:570`

## tech_wormhole_generation_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

要创建稳定的虫洞需要花费时间和大量的能源，但是这个过程可以通过使用一种特殊的校准仪来优化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

The creation of a stable wormhole takes time and requires massive amounts of energy, but the process can be streamlined with the use of special calibrators.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:571`

## tech_wormhole_generation_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_3&lang=en)

### 中文

虫洞控制场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Wormhole Containment Fields

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:572`

## tech_wormhole_generation_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_generation_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些控制场允许我们在无惧触发突如其来的亚空间能量串级的风险下，创造更强大的虫洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

These containment fields allow for the creation of more powerful wormholes without the risk of triggering a sudden subspace energy cascade.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:573`

## tech_hyper_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_1&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间移动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Hyperspace Travel

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:574`

## tech_hyper_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像蜘蛛网上的丝线一样，多维空间的其他维度在大多数恒星的重力井之间运动。在这些超空间航道间理论上是可以进行超光速旅行的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Like the strands of a spider web, the extra-dimensional realm of hyperspace runs between the gravity wells of most stars. Faster than light travel is theoretically possible along these hyperlanes.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:575`

## tech_hyper_drive_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_2&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间航道突破点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Hyperlane Breach Points

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:576`

## tech_hyper_drive_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经确定太空中有能使舰船消耗更少的能源“突破”并进入一个超空间航道的点的存在。这将产生新一代的超级引擎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

We have identified the existence of points in space where a ship expends less energy to "breach" their way into a hyperlane. This will lead to a new generation of hyper drives.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:577`

## tech_hyper_drive_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_3&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间滑流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Hyperspace Slipstreams

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:578`

## tech_hyper_drive_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyper_drive_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新一代的超级引擎能够通过超空间中存在的亚空间滑流。这使得引擎的性能显着提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

A new generation of hyper drives capable of accessing the subspace slipstreams that are present within hyperspace. This leads to dramatically improved drive performance.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:579`

## tech_hyperlane_mapping

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyperlane_mapping&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间航道测绘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

Hyperlane Mapping

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:580`

## tech_hyperlane_mapping_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hyperlane_mapping_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们不通过超空间旅行，但绘制星系宽广的超空间航道网络在我们对付那些使用航道的人时将带来明显的优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Although we do not travel through hyperspace, mapping the galaxy's extensive hyperlane network gives us a clear advantage against those who do.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:581`

## tech_warp_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_1&lang=en)

### 中文

曲速移动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Warp Travel

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:582`

## tech_warp_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

用曲速场包裹舰船，形成一个亚空间泡泡，理论上来说就可以超越光速了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

By enveloping a vessel in a warp field and forming a subspace bubble around it, velocities exceeding the speed of light are theoretically possible.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:583`

## tech_warp_drive_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_2&lang=en)

### 中文

曲速场稳定器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Warp Field Stabilizers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:584`

## tech_warp_drive_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改进的曲速场稳定技术将使我们的舰船航行更远的同时花费更少的能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Improved stabilization techniques for warp fields will enable our ships to travel farther while expending less energy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:585`

## tech_warp_drive_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_3&lang=en)

### 中文

曲速引擎冲压发动机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Warp Drive Ramscoops

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:586`

## tech_warp_drive_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_warp_drive_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些革命性的冲压发动机装在曲速引擎上起到粒子收集器的作用，不断补充舰船的燃料供应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

These revolutionary ramscoops are attached to warp drives and function as particle collectors, replenishing a starship's fuel supply.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:587`

## tech_jump_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_jump_drive_1&lang=en)

### 中文

跃迁引擎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Jump Drive

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:588`

## tech_jump_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_jump_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一项开创性科技奇迹，跃迁引擎可以粉碎当地的时空连续性并在量子层面上重组至与目标地点完全相同的状态，于是这艘舰船基几乎在一瞬间完成了星系间的“跃迁”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

A groundbreaking technological marvel, the Jump Drive shreds the local space-time continuum and rearranges it on the quantum level to be identical to that of the target destination - and thus the ship appears to near-instantly "jump" from system to system.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:589`

## tech_psi_jump_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psi_jump_drive_1&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能跃迁引擎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Psi Jump Drive

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:590`

## tech_psi_jump_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psi_jump_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能跳跃引擎利用船员放射的灵能使舰体穿越现实的空间结构，立即“跳跃”过超长的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

The Psi Jump Drive harnesses the psi energies emitted by the crew to force the vessel through the very fabric of reality, instantly "jumping" vast distances.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:591`

## tech_wormhole_stabilization

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_stabilization&lang=en)

### 中文

虫洞稳定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Wormhole Stabilization

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:592`

## tech_wormhole_stabilization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_wormhole_stabilization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

天然产生的虫洞变幻无常，而其波动的能级通常使舰船无法安全地穿行其连接的开口。不过，暂时稳定其基体的方法还是存在的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

A naturally occurring subspace wormhole is a fickle thing, and its fluctuating energy levels typically make it impossible to travel safely between its linked apertures. There are ways to temporarily stabilize its matrix, however.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:593`

## tech_gateway_activation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gateway_activation&lang=en)

### 中文

星门激活

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Gateway Activation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:594`

## tech_gateway_activation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gateway_activation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

激活在星系中四散的废弃星门空间站的能力，可以极大地缩短星际旅行的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

The ability to reactivate the derelict Gateway stations that can be found scattered throughout the galaxy has the potential to dramatically shorten travel times.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:595`

## tech_gateway_construction

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gateway_construction&lang=en)

### 中文

星门建造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Gateway Construction

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:596`

## tech_gateway_construction_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gateway_construction_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建造新的星门空间站使我们可以建立一个快速通行网络，给星际通行带来革命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

The construction of new Gateway stations enables us to build a fast-travel network that could revolutionize interstellar travel.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:597`

## tech_shield_recharge_aura_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_shield_recharge_aura_1&lang=en)

### 中文

电容场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Capacitor Fields

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:600`

## tech_shield_recharge_aura_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_shield_recharge_aura_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

限域性带电粒子场可以为耗尽的护盾电容充能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

Localized fields of charged particles can energize depleted shield capacitors.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:601`

## tech_aura_shield_dampener

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_aura_shield_dampener&lang=en)

### 中文

护盾阻尼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Shield Dampeners

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:602`

## tech_aura_quantum_destabilizer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_aura_quantum_destabilizer&lang=en)

### 中文

量子去稳器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Quantum Destabilizer

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:604`

## tech_aura_quantum_destabilizer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_aura_quantum_destabilizer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

量子去稳器可以扰动局部的量子场，干扰敌方舰船上敏感的定位设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

Quantum Destabilizers agitate the local quantum field, disturbing sensitive targeting equipment aboard hostile ships.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:605`

## tech_enigmatic_disruption_field

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_disruption_field&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘干扰力场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Enigmatic Disruption Field

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:606`

## tech_enigmatic_disruption_field_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_disruption_field_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

向一个局部区域灌输随机调制的弱电磁脉冲，敌人的护盾和武器系统会受到部分干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

By flooding a localized area with weak, randomly-modulated electromagnetic pulses, enemy shield and weapons systems can be partially disrupted.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:607`

## tech_enigmatic_encoder

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_encoder&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘编码器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

Enigmatic Encoder

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:610`

## tech_enigmatic_encoder_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_encoder_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘编码器在将飞行路径数据返回舰队指挥中心前，会根据某种未知的法则对数据进行加密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

The Enigmatic Encoder scrambles flight path data according to some indeterminable design before feeding it back to fleet command.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:611`

## tech_enigmatic_decoder

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_decoder&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘解码器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Enigmatic Decoder

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:612`

## tech_enigmatic_decoder_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_enigmatic_decoder_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然神秘解码器在标准测试中表现平平，但目标的飞行路径越不规则，该解码器预测算法的准确度似乎就越高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

While producing average results in standard tests, the accuracy of Enigmatic Decoder's prediction algorithms seemingly increases as the target's flight path grows more erratic.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:613`

## tech_ascension_theory

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ascension_theory&lang=en)

### 中文

飞升理论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Ascension Theory

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:616`

## tech_ascension_theory_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ascension_theory_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的历史就是一个不断螺旋上升的过程。每次循环中，我们的科技、政治和文化水平都会出现飞跃式的发展。这些飞跃通常都出现在动乱之后，随后又带来相对平稳的时代。通过甄别这样的核心前提，我们也许能够大大缩短循环的时间，这样有利于缩短我们的发展周期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Our history is a repeating cycle of dramatic technological, political, and cultural leaps; these leaps are often preceded by periods of upheaval and followed by times of relative stability. By identifying the core, recurring premises of these leaps, we may be able to effectively short-circuit the cycle for our own benefit.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:617`

## tech_lasers_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_1&lang=en)

### 中文

红激光发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Red Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:626`

## tech_lasers_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

激光武器可以向目标发射聚焦光束，通过高热量给敌方造成伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Laser weapons fire focused beams of light at targets, causing damage through the generation of intense heat.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:627`

## tech_lasers_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_2&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝激光发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

Blue Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:628`

## tech_lasers_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

比红色激光器更加强大，对有机生物来说，这种波长的电磁辐射看上去是蓝色或紫色的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

More powerful than red lasers, these blue variants emit electromagnetic radiation at a wavelength that appears blue or violet to most organics.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:629`

## tech_lasers_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_3&lang=en)

### 中文

紫外激光发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

UV Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:630`

## tech_lasers_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

紫外激光器是它的前身蓝激光器的更新强化版本。这些武器精准而充满毁灭性，可以造成巨大的杀伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

Ultraviolet lasers are an updated and more powerful version of their blue predecessors. Accurate and destructive, these weapons are capable of inflicting great damage.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:631`

## tech_lasers_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_4&lang=en)

### 中文

X射线发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

X-Ray Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:632`

## tech_lasers_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种激光的波长较紫外线短一些，但是更具毁灭性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

These lasers have a shorter wavelength than UV-based laser weaponry, but are even more destructive.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:633`

## tech_lasers_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_5&lang=en)

### 中文

伽马激光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Gamma Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:634`

## tech_lasers_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_lasers_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

伽马激光的电磁辐射频率极高。在激光武器领域，其破坏力是无可比拟的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

The electromagnetic radiation emitted by gamma lasers is of an extremely high frequency. Their destructive potential is unmatched in the field of laser weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:635`

## tech_energy_lance_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_lance_1&lang=en)

### 中文

粒子光矛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

Particle Lances

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:636`

## tech_energy_lance_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_lance_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些重型能量武器能够发射聚焦的粒子射线并造成巨幅伤害。发射阵列的规模限制了它们只能运用在战列舰和泰坦上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

These heavy energy weapons fire focused particle beams that cause massive damage. The size of the emitter arrays limits their use to battleships and titans.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:637`

## tech_energy_lance_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_lance_2&lang=en)

### 中文

快子光矛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

Tachyon Lances

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:638`

## tech_energy_lance_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_lance_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

粒子光矛更为强力的版本，该武器发射携带有巨大能量的快子光束。和它的之前版本一样，只能运用在战列舰和泰坦上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

A more powerful version of the particle lance, this weapon fires a tachyon beam of immense power. Like its predecessor, its use is limited to battleships and titans.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:639`

## tech_perdition_beam

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_perdition_beam&lang=en)

### 中文

炼狱射线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

Perdition Beam

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:640`

## tech_perdition_beam_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_perdition_beam_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个巨大的串联能源增幅导管阵列，可以发射一道威力前所未有的光束。它的尺寸意味着只有泰坦级星舰才能装得上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

A massive array of linked energy amplification conduits that fires a beam of unprecedented firepower. Its size means that it can only be fitted on Titan-class starships.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:641`

## tech_plasma_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_1&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Plasma Throwers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:644`

## tech_plasma_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可以向目标发射毁灭性的高能等离子球体的武器。这种等离子抛射体在对付大型舰船的装甲时特别有效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

Weapons that eject destructive balls of high-energy plasma at targets. These plasma projectiles are extremely effective at eating through ship armor.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:645`

## tech_plasma_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_2&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子加速器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

Plasma Accelerators

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:646`

## tech_plasma_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种新的加速炮利发射具有更先进约束场的高能等离子体。这种技术可以降低等离子体向目标飞行过程中所逸散的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

These new accelerators launch projectiles of high-energy plasma with improved containment fields. This results in less energy leakage as the projectile travels in space towards its target.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:647`

## tech_plasma_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_3&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子加农炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Plasma Cannons

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:648`

## tech_plasma_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_plasma_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最先进的等离子武器，这种火炮是之前的升级版等离子发射器完全军事化的成果。它们的等离子抛射体更加致命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

The latest in plasma weaponry, these cannons are fully militarized versions of the older, somewhat improvised plasma throwers. Their plasma projectiles are even deadlier.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:649`

## tech_arc_emitter_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_arc_emitter_1&lang=en)

### 中文

电弧发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

Arc Emitters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:650`

## tech_arc_emitter_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_arc_emitter_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该武器可以向目标发射带有混乱能量的电弧。可以在某种程度上无视装甲和护盾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

Powerful weapons that launch a lightning arc of chaotic energy at targets, which in part can ignore armor and shields.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:651`

## tech_arc_emitter_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_arc_emitter_2&lang=en)

### 中文

聚能电弧发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

Focused Arc Emitters

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:652`

## tech_arc_emitter_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_arc_emitter_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些升级后的发射器装备了更优秀的聚焦透镜，这使得它们可以发射出更加强大的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

These upgraded emitters are equipped with better focusing lenses, resulting in a more powerful energy discharge.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:653`

## tech_disruptors_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_1&lang=en)

### 中文

裂解炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

Disruptors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:656`

## tech_disruptors_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

裂解炮发射出的高能射束，能够同时弱化并破坏构成目标原子间的分子键。它们能够穿透护盾和护甲，对敌方的船体和船员直接造成毁灭性的打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

Disruptors fire high-energy bolts that weaken and destroy the molecular bonds that hold the target's constituent atoms together. They are capable of passing through shields and armor to wreak havoc directly on enemy hull and crew.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:657`

## tech_disruptors_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_2&lang=en)

### 中文

离子裂解炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

Ion Disruptors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:658`

## tech_disruptors_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些升级版的裂解炮可以对目标发射出更具威力的离子流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

These upgraded disruptors fire bolts of more destructive ion particles at targets.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:659`

## tech_disruptors_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_3&lang=en)

### 中文

相位裂解炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

Phase Disruptors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:660`

## tech_disruptors_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_disruptors_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种极限强化版的裂解炮能够发射量子能，任何被命中的目标都会承受极大的伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Extremely high-yield disruptors that fire quantum energy charges causing incredible damage to those unfortunate enough to get in their way.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:661`

## tech_energy_torpedoes_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_torpedoes_1&lang=en)

### 中文

质子发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:601`

### English

Proton Launchers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:662`

## tech_energy_torpedoes_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_torpedoes_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些能量抛射体由紧密聚集的质子组成，能够对敌方船体造成巨额伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

These energy projectiles consist of tightly concentrated protons capable of causing immense damage to the hull of enemy ships.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:663`

## tech_energy_torpedoes_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_torpedoes_2&lang=en)

### 中文

中子发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:603`

### English

Neutron Launchers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:664`

## tech_energy_torpedoes_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_energy_torpedoes_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于中子具有毁灭性的能量，升级的能量发射物将依赖中子而非质子对敌方进行打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:604`

### English

Upgraded energy projectiles that rely on neutrons instead of protons for their destructive power.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:665`

## null_void_beam

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anull_void_beam&lang=en)

### 中文

太虚光束

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Null Void Beam

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:666`

## null_void_beam_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anull_void_beam_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用太虚的熵特性，这种光束可以真正地摧毁能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

By harnessing the entropic nature of the Null Void, this beam can actually destroy energy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:667`

## tech_extradimensional_weapon_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_extradimensional_weapon_1&lang=en)

### 中文

高维武器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

Extradimensional Weaponry

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:670`

## tech_extradimensional_weapon_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_extradimensional_weapon_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在低次元中使用第五维的能量操纵原理，可以造就强大的能量武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

Applying 5th-dimensional energy manipulation principles on lower dimensional planes allows for the creation of powerful energy weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:671`

## tech_mining_drone_weapon_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_drone_weapon_1&lang=en)

### 中文

采矿无人机激光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Mining Drone Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:672`

## tech_mining_drone_weapon_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mining_drone_weapon_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用高度聚焦透镜结合振荡发射频率来创造出足够开采小行星或击落敌人的激光。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

Using intense-focus lenses in combination with an oscillating firing frequency help create lasers strong enough to mine asteroids, or shoot down hostiles.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:673`

## tech_space_cloud_weapon_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_cloud_weapon_1&lang=en)

### 中文

雷云导线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

Cloud Lightning Conduits

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:674`

## tech_space_cloud_weapon_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_cloud_weapon_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之云可以通过构成其核心的大部分的密集气体所产生的电荷来释放出燃烧的等离子体电弧。按照此原理，利用相同成分的气体配合上导管即可以制造出威力强大的闪电武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Void Clouds use the electrical charges generated by the dense gases that make up most of their core to release bolts of burning plasma. Using the same gas composition together with a specialized conduit allows for powerful lightning weaponry.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:675`

## tech_blue_crystal_weapon_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_blue_crystal_weapon_1&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝水晶电容

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

Blue Crystal Capacitors

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:676`

## tech_blue_crystal_weapon_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_blue_crystal_weapon_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态实体以其棱镜状的躯体储存宇宙射线，并将其聚焦并以类似激光一样的光束形式释放。基于这种蓝色的棱镜模板，我们可以造出能产生相似波长能量的激光电容器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

Crystalline Entities store stellar radiation within their prismatic bodies and can focus it to emit laser-like beams. Using the sapphire type's prismatic pattern as a base we can construct laser capacitors that produce energy at a similar wavelength.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:677`

## tech_green_crystal_weapon_1

## tech_hydroponics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hydroponics&lang=en)

### 中文

水培农业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

Hydroponics Farming

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:690`

## tech_hydroponics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hydroponics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在受控制的生态环境下进行水培耕作可使谷物在正常来说不可能农作的地方生长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

Hydroponics Farming in a controlled environment allows for crops to be grown in climates where farming would normally be impossible.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:691`

## tech_industrial_farming

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_industrial_farming&lang=en)

### 中文

农业工业化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

Industrial Agriculture

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:692`

## tech_industrial_farming_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_industrial_farming_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过大规模工业化农场生产富含养分的农作物，可以让一个文明的人口成倍地增长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

Large-scale industrial farms growing nutrient-rich produce allows a civilization to grow its population manifold.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:693`

## tech_eco_simulation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_eco_simulation&lang=en)

### 中文

生态模拟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

Eco Simulation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:694`

## tech_eco_simulation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_eco_simulation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过电脑来模拟各种环境与气候条件，可以使作物的产量更加可控。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Farming techniques can be further refined through sophisticated computer simulations of different environments and weather patterns. This allows for more predictable crop yields.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:695`

## tech_gene_crops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gene_crops&lang=en)

### 中文

转基因作物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

Gene Crops

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:696`

## tech_gene_crops_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_gene_crops_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成熟的作物基因工程有助于进一步提高产量并改善口味和营养价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

Sophisticated genetic engineering of crops helps increase yield and further improves both taste and nutrient levels.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:697`

## tech_nano_vitality_crops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nano_vitality_crops&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米活性作物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

Nano-Vitality Crops

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:698`

## tech_nano_vitality_crops_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nano_vitality_crops_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在作物中融入少量的纳米机器人帮助我们创造出了一种具有极强恢复力的作物，其化学组成在整个生命周期都可以进行定制和改变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Infusing crop strands with small amounts of nano-machines help create a type of produce that is ultra resilient, and the chemical composition of which can be tailored and altered during its entire lifetime.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:699`

## tech_nutrient_replication

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nutrient_replication&lang=en)

### 中文

营养复制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

Nutrient Replication

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:702`

## tech_nutrient_replication_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nutrient_replication_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然合成复制作物需要大量收集废料才能产生少量农产品，但这一过程仍对农业产量产生了重要的推动作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

The process of synthetically replicating crops requires a lot of collected waste to create a small amount of produce, but nevertheless provides a significant boost to agricultural output.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:703`

## tech_engos_vapor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engos_vapor&lang=en)

### 中文

精炼恩吉欧气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Engos Vapor Refining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:706`

## tech_engos_vapor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_engos_vapor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种天然存在的兴奋剂，帮助所有生命适应并演化。它也可以作为一个非常强力的能量武器导体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

A naturally occurring stimulant that helps all forms of life adapt and evolve. It can also be used as a very potent conductor for energy weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:707`

## tech_pitharan_dust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_pitharan_dust&lang=en)

### 中文

精炼皮萨兰星尘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Pitharan Dust Refining

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:708`

## tech_pitharan_dust_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_pitharan_dust_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在某些星球发现的这种粉尘是一种天然的兴奋剂。它对大多数人有镇静作用，可以作为一个非武力的方式安抚敌对的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

This fine dust is a naturally occurring stimulant found on some worlds. It has a calming effect on most beings, and can be used as a non-violent way of pacifying hostile individuals.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:709`

## tech_society_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_1&lang=en)

### 中文

异星生物学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Xenobiology

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:725`

## tech_society_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对外星生命的研究已经超越了单纯的编目。通过研究在外星球上演化的生物体的结构和生存策略，我们在医学、适应和设计方面开辟了新的途径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

The study of alien life has moved beyond mere cataloging. By examining the structures and survival strategies of organisms evolved under foreign stars, we open new avenues in medicine, adaptation, and design.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:726`

## tech_society_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_2&lang=en)

### 中文

神经编织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

Neural Weaving

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:727`

## tech_society_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过递归轴突支架和突触网，我们开发了重组意识物质的电化学拓扑结构的方法。神经元不只是传递信息——它们还会缠绕、组织和记忆。现在，它们是按照我们的设计来运行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

Through recursive axonal scaffolding and synaptic reticulation, we have developed methods for reorganizing the electrochemical topology of conscious matter. Neurons do not merely transmit - they entwine, organize, and remember. Now, they do so according to our design.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:728`

## tech_society_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_3&lang=en)

### 中文

生物构造合成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Biostructural Synthesis

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:729`

## tech_society_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_society_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生命是由重复的模式和模块化结构组成的，具有令人瞠目结舌的多样性。将其解码并在每个尺度上加以复制，我们就掌握了生物学的整体蓝图。自然界中我们所不了解的已经所剩无几，我们无法再造的事物就更少了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Life, in all its staggering variety, is composed of repeating patterns and modular structures. By decoding and replicating these at every scale, we now wield a holistic blueprint of biology. There is little left in nature we do not understand, and less still we cannot recreate.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:730`

## tech_alien_life_studies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_alien_life_studies&lang=en)

### 中文

异种生物学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Xenology

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:740`

## tech_alien_life_studies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_alien_life_studies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究其他星球的动物群为我们的外星生物行为学研究提供了一些新见解，这使得我们可以识别和驯养温顺的个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Studying the fauna of other worlds gives insight into alien behaviors, allowing us to identify and domesticate docile entities.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:741`

## tech_xeno_zoo

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_zoo&lang=en)

### 中文

异星动物园

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Xeno Zoo

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:742`

## tech_xeno_zoo_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_zoo_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究各种外星生命形态并对它们的潜在风险有了清晰的认识后，我们将能够收集活体样本并保持展出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

Having studied various alien lifeforms and developed a clear understanding of the potential risks inherent in containing them, we would be able to collect living specimens to keep on display.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:743`

## tech_colonization_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_1&lang=en)

### 中文

新世界协议

### English

New Worlds Protocol

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:744`

## tech_colonization_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

计算得出建立一个行星殖民地所需的资源和设备后，我们勇敢的先驱者将驾驶大殖民船外出探索，相信他们会在远方的行星上建立新的家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Calculating required resources and gear required to establish a planetary colony allows us to herd brave pioneers into grand colony ships, confident that they will survive the long voyage across the stars to a new home.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:745`

## tech_colonization_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_2&lang=en)

### 中文

大气过滤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

Atmospheric Filtering

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:746`

## tech_colonization_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因地制宜地调节大气过滤可以更好地过滤掉有害的元素，以期改善殖民地的生活条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

By adjusting atmospheric filtering on a per-site basis to better filter out undesirable elements, colonial living conditions may be improved.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:747`

## tech_colonization_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步研究其他行星上发现的危险环境有助于缓解殖民者在其他星球的日常生活中遇到的困难。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Further study into the hazardous environments found on other planets help alleviate the difficulties that colonizers encounter in their day-to-day lives on foreign worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:749`

## tech_colonization_4

### English

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:750`

## tech_colonization_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对多种土壤的种类及其养育作物的能力进行的大量研究使我们可以改善殖民者在其他星球上的生存条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Extensive study into different soil types and their ability to support crops will help improve the living conditions of our settlers on foreign worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:751`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_5&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:752`

## tech_colonization_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_colonization_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对不同行星气候的研究能够让我们的人口定居在过去我们认为过于严酷的世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Additional study into different planetary climates will allow us to settle our population on worlds previously thought too hostile to support them.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:753`

### 中文

对经过辐射的环境进行综合研究后，我们可以在被核战争摧毁的星球上进行殖民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

## tech_frontier_health_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_frontier_health_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

组装新机械单位时，使用模块化部件不仅能提高组装效率，还使得未来的维修工作变得更加容易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

The use of modular parts when assembling new machine units will both streamline the process and make future repairs easier.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:797`

## tech_frontier_hospital

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_frontier_hospital&lang=en)

### 中文

细胞修复

### English

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:798`

## tech_frontier_hospital_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_frontier_hospital_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着帝国日益强大，我们对生物学也更加了解。先进的细胞层面的医疗手段可以使生物人口的健康状况大幅提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

As our empire grows, so does our understanding of biology. Advanced medical treatments on a cellular level have allowed for significant advancements in biological population health.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:799`

## tech_frontier_hospital_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_frontier_hospital_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

## tech_frontier_hospital_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_frontier_hospital_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

这些工厂其实是有自我意识的个体，只需中央机械智能提供少量信息就能够不断优化自身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

These factories are essentially self-aware entities that continually optimize themselves with only minimal input from the central Machine Intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:801`

### 中文

地表重塑
