### English

Planetary Resurfacing

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:806`

## tech_mountain_range_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_mountain_range_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着无污染核聚变、自动化以及物质压缩技术的出现，从铲除山顶发展到铲除整段山脉只是时间问题了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

With the advent of clean fusion, automation, and matter compression technologies, it was only a matter of time before mountain top removal became mountain range removal.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:807`

## tech_volcano

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_volcano&lang=en)

### 中文

地壳深部工程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

Deep Crust Engineering

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:808`

## tech_volcano_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_volcano_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

材料科技的发展与深井开采技术可使我们在过去无法想象的深度与温度中开展地质工程。岩浆热点现在已可以进行开发，抑制甚至迁移。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Advances in material technology and deep-shaft mining techniques allow geo-engineering at previously unthinkable depths and temperatures. Magma hotspots can now be exploited, suppressed, or even relocated.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:809`

## tech_dangerous_wildlife

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dangerous_wildlife&lang=en)

### 中文

清除危险野生动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

Dangerous Wildlife Removal

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:810`

## tech_dangerous_wildlife_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dangerous_wildlife_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

破坏自然栖息地配合围剿扫荡可以有效地在短时间内清除危险的野生动物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Tactical disruption of natural habitats combined with converging search-and-destroy patterns can effectively eliminate hostile wildlife in a very short span of time.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:811`

## tech_dense_jungle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dense_jungle&lang=en)

### 中文

选择性脱叶剂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

Selective Defoliants

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:812`

## tech_dense_jungle_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_dense_jungle_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过编目分类、基因测序以及用计算机模拟外星植物生态体系，配合基因编辑与传播机理，我们可以按自己的喜好改造外星植物群落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

Thorough cataloging, gene sequencing, and computer modeling of exobotanical ecosystems, coupled with gene editing and dispersal mechanisms, will allow us to reshape alien plant communities to our liking.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:813`

## tech_quicksand_basin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_quicksand_basin&lang=en)

### 中文

土壤修复

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

Soil Remediation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:814`

## tech_quicksand_basin_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_quicksand_basin_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

土壤沉积过程通常需要数百万年的时间。应用先进的水力压裂技术、化学工程以及液压管理科技，即使最贫瘠的地层也能变成肥沃的表层土。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

The process of soil deposition normally takes millions of years. With advanced fracking, chemical engineering, and hydraulic management technologies even the poorest substrate can be turned into fertile topsoil.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:815`

## tech_noxious_swamp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_noxious_swamp&lang=en)

### 中文

异种水力掌控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

Xeno-Hydraulic Mastery

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:816`

## tech_noxious_swamp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_noxious_swamp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

智慧物种从数千年前就已经开始开垦沼地。通过模块化引水渠、水泵站、化学整治装置与自动土壤压缩机之间的协调配合，我们能以前所未有的速度开垦沼地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

Sapient races have been reclaiming swampland for thousands of years. A coordinated system of modular aqueducts, pump stations, chemical remediation crawlers and automated soil compactors can reclaim land at unprecedented speeds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:817`

## tech_massive_glacier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_massive_glacier&lang=en)

### 中文

气候控制网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

Climate Control Network

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:818`

## tech_massive_glacier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_massive_glacier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要管理得当，轨道折射镜与遮蔽装置系统可以大幅影响星球的气候。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Orbital systems of mirrors and shades can effect dramatic changes in planetary climates, when managed properly.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:819`

## tech_toxic_kelp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_toxic_kelp&lang=en)

### 中文

海洋生态管理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

Ocean Ecology Management

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:820`

## tech_toxic_kelp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_toxic_kelp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

只要有足够的处理能力、传感器网络以及分站，即使洋流也能在一定程度上实现精确控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Given enough processing power, networked sensors and distributed stations, even the ocean currents can be managed to a fine level of control.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:821`

## tech_deep_sinkhole

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_deep_sinkhole&lang=en)

### 中文

地下殖民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Subterranean Colonization

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:822`

## tech_deep_sinkhole_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_deep_sinkhole_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多星球都有着巨大的地下空洞。地下探索、开发以及建造技术可以极大地增加某些星球的可居住空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

Many planets exhibit extensive subsurface voids. Subterranean exploration, exploitation, and construction techniques can greatly increase the habitable area of some worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:823`

## tech_space_trading

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_trading&lang=en)

### 中文

外星贸易公司

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Offworld Trade Companies

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:826`

## tech_space_trading_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_trading_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

跨星际的贸易需要我们克服新的一系列的挑战。以及时且最具成本效益的方式将货物从一个星系运输到另一个星系是至关重要的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

Trade across interstellar distances presents a new set of challenges to overcome. It is critical to achieve the transfer of goods from one system to another in a timely and above all cost efficient manner.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:827`

## tech_terrestrial_sculpting

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_terrestrial_sculpting&lang=en)

### 中文

地表塑造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Terrestrial Sculpting

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:830`

## tech_terrestrial_sculpting_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_terrestrial_sculpting_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

重塑宏观尺度的行星地壳的问题没有简单的解决方法——只有更大的铲子和轨道部署级钻头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

There is no simple solution to the problem of macro-scale reshaping of planetary crusts - only bigger trowels and orbitally-deployed drills.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:831`

## tech_atmospheric_manipulation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_atmospheric_manipulation&lang=en)

### 中文

大气操控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

Atmospheric Manipulation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:832`

## tech_atmospheric_manipulation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_atmospheric_manipulation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

湿度调节器和化学扩散装置是从根本上改变大气的第一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

Humidity regulators and chemical dispersal units are the first step towards a fundamentally altered atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:833`

## tech_ecological_adaptation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ecological_adaptation&lang=en)

### 中文

生态适应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

Ecological Adaptation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:834`

## tech_ecological_adaptation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ecological_adaptation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过改造行星生态系统使其更能适应大规模环境变化，我们能更有条不紊的进行环境改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

By adapting planetary ecosystems to large-scale environmental changes we can turn terraformation into a less disruptive process.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:835`

## tech_climate_restoration

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_climate_restoration&lang=en)

### 中文

气候复苏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

Climate Restoration

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:836`

## tech_climate_restoration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_climate_restoration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过提升我们对大气改造的理解，我们将能对大多数的生态荒芜的世界进行环境改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

By advancing our understanding of atmospheric transformation, we will be able to terraform even the most ecologically devastated worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:837`

## tech_volcanic_terraforming

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_volcanic_terraforming&lang=en)

### 中文

火山环境改造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Volcanic Terraforming

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:838`

## tech_volcanic_terraforming_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_volcanic_terraforming_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

材料科技与深井开采技术的进步使地质工程能达到前所未有的深度和温度。我们能将火山星球环境改造成温带星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Advances in material technology and deep-shaft mining techniques allow geo-engineering at previously unthinkable depths and temperatures. We can terraform a Volcanic World into a temperate planet.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:839`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_1

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着系外远征变得更加普遍，为归来的舰队提供行星侧的补给非常重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

As extrasolar expeditions become more common, it is important to provide planetside relief for returning fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:908`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_2&lang=en)

### English

Doctrine: Support Vessels

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:909`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支舰队的运行不能仅凭一腔热血，为了确保能稳定地获得重要的补给，我们必须将后勤军队组织成专门的支援舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

A fleet does not run on zeal alone. To ensure steady access to critical supplies, we should organize our logistics corps into dedicated support vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:910`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_3&lang=en)

### 中文

学说：星际后勤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Doctrine: Interstellar Logistics

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:911`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新手谈论战术，专家研究后勤。我们可以通过确保舰队能够定期而高效地获得补给，来增加我们能够有效地支持的舰船数量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

Amateurs talk about tactics. Professionals study logistics. By ensuring our fleets are regularly and efficiently supplied, we can increase the number of vessels we can effectively support.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:912`

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_4&lang=en)

### English

## tech_doctrine_navy_size_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_doctrine_navy_size_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过维持一支专业的舰队联络队伍，我们可以改善指挥链中的命令传达与通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

By maintaining a professional corps of fleet liaisons, we can improve communications and the flow of orders throughout the chain of command.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:914`

### 中文

升级和改造船部件的标准化工作流程能促进大规模的效率提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Standardizing workflows for upgrading and retrofitting ship components can lead to massive efficiency gains.

## tech_centralized_command_desc_hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_centralized_command_desc_hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

通过建立训练设施，自主子个体可以学习如何指挥低等同胞，这样可以获得更高的战场灵活性和效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

By establishing training facilities where the autonomous drones can learn to command their lesser brethren, we will achieve greater battlefield flexibility and effectiveness.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:920`

## tech_centralized_command

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_centralized_command&lang=en)

### English

## tech_centralized_command_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_centralized_command_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为各级军事行动打造专门的指挥部以形成高效的舰队，为未来的军事训练打下良好的基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

Creating dedicated headquarters for all levels of military operations shapes an efficient fleet, and lays a better foundation for future military training.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:922`

## tech_centralized_command_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_centralized_command_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

维护指挥所

### English

Maintenance Command

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:923`

## tech_centralized_command_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_centralized_command_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

所有机械都需要维护，对战体而言尤为甚至。专门的维修团队可以将磨损维持到最低，并减少修理费用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

All machines require maintenance, and this is especially true for warforms. A dedicated maintenance corps will keep attritional breakdowns to a minimum and decrease repair costs.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:924`

## tech_combat_training

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_combat_training&lang=en)

### English

Combat Training

## tech_combat_training_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_combat_training_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是为新兵准备的更完善的培训计划，包括现实的战斗场景，让他们做好进入战场的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

A more extensive training program for new recruits, including realistic combat scenarios, prepare them for the field.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:926`

## tech_combat_training_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_combat_training_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

战地维修

### English

Field Repairs

## tech_combat_training_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_combat_training_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

为我们的地面作战单位配备技术数据库，他们就可以在战场上进行诊断和有限的自我维修。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

By equipping our combat ground units with technical databases, they can perform diagnostics and limited self-repair in the field.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:928`

## tech_ground_defense_planning

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ground_defense_planning&lang=en)

### 中文

地面防御计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

Ground Defense Planning

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:929`

## tech_ground_defense_planning_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ground_defense_planning_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有很多外星帝国希望将我们的星球据为己有。我们必须要做好万全的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

There are many alien empires who would gladly seize our worlds for themselves. We must be prepared for them, should the worst come to pass.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:930`

## tech_global_defense_grid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_global_defense_grid&lang=en)

### 中文

全球防御网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Global Defense Grid

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:931`

## tech_global_defense_grid_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_global_defense_grid_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

良好的防御在今天的重要程度丝毫不逊于我们祖先所生活的中古时代。技术的进步不会使防御工事过时，只会改变它们的存在形式和强度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

The strategic value of a good defense remains as relevant today as it was to our predecessors in the far distant past. Technology does not render fortifications obsolete - it merely changes their requirements.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:932`

## tech_psionic_theory

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psionic_theory&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能理论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Psionic Theory

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:935`

## tech_psionic_theory_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psionic_theory_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

心灵往往是宇宙自身的映射，也是一个小宇宙。并不是谁都会触及到如此深层的洞见，但他们的心灵却会是无上的器具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

The mind is in many ways a reflection of the universe itself, a microcosmos. Not all beings will reach this level of insight, but their minds are tools greater than any other.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:936`

### English

## tech_telepathy_machine_gestalt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_telepathy_machine_gestalt_desc&lang=en)

## tech_precognition_interface_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_precognition_interface_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不断探索灵能领域，读取心灵波动先于获得实际感知，从而制造出预知接口作战计算机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

Advances in the field of Psionics suggest the possibility of creating combat computers capable of reading and analyzing the impulses of telepathic operatives before they are cognitively processed.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:948`

## tech_psi_jump_drives

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psi_jump_drives&lang=en)

### English

## tech_psi_jump_drives_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_psi_jump_drives_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能跳跃引擎利用船员放射的灵能使舰体穿越现实的空间结构，立即“跳跃”过超长的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:878`

### English

The Psi Jump Drive harnesses the psi energies emitted by the crew to force the vessel through the very fabric of reality, instantly "jumping" vast distances.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:950`

## tech_xeno_outreach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_outreach&lang=en)

异星扩张

### English

Xeno Outreach

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:953`

## tech_xeno_outreach_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_outreach_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星际贸易表面上与一般交易类似，但其中错综复杂却远胜于之，任何土著文明的贸易史都相形见绌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

The intricacies of interstellar trade closely resemble that of any other transaction, but is performed on a scale dwarfing the entire history of trade for most pre-FTL civilizations.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:954`

## tech_xeno_relations

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_relations&lang=en)

### 中文

异星关系学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

Xeno Relations

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:955`

## tech_xeno_relations_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_relations_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于星际外交极度复杂，为了达成自身目标，必须与其他势力结交。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

The complexities of interstellar diplomacy necessitate the forming of bonds with foreign powers in order to further one's goals.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:956`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_supremacy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

踏足星系浊流的唯一目标即登上巅峰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

We navigate a minefield of galactic power dynamics with a singular purpose; to come out on top.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:958`

## tech_xeno_diplomacy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_xeno_diplomacy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

理解如何影响和操纵异种，是建立长久星际联盟的必由之路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Advancing our understanding of interactions with - and manipulations of - the xeno is a necessary step in forming more permanent interstellar alliances.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:960`

## tech_federation_code

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_federation_code&lang=en)

### English

## tech_federation_code_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_federation_code_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个联邦不仅仅是善意和共同的雄心。这份准则为统一的星际秩序奠定了基础，确保其成员之间的合作与稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

A Federation must be more than goodwill and shared ambition. This code provides the groundwork for a unified interstellar order, ensuring cooperation and stability among its members.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:962`

## tech_interstellar_campaigns

## tech_interstellar_campaigns_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_interstellar_campaigns_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

枪杆子里面出政权。强者为所欲为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

A well-oiled war machine is the key to exerting influence on a galactic scale. Backed by the might of our military, we can lay claim to almost anything we want.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:969`

## tech_galactic_campaigns

### English

## tech_galactic_campaigns_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_galactic_campaigns_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改革政体，增强军备，从而无远弗届。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Further improvements to administration grant an extended power projection across the stars, and the military clout needed to enforce our demands.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:971`

## tech_existential_campaigns

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_existential_campaigns&lang=en)

### 中文

存亡运动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

Existential Campaigns

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:972`

## tech_existential_campaigns_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_existential_campaigns_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当生存本身受到威胁时，战争这一概念便失去了意义。这些战役的发动并非为了统治，而是为了确保我们文明的存续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

When survival itself is at stake, the very concept of war loses its meaning. These campaigns are waged not for dominion, but to secure the continued existence of our civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:973`

## tech_existential_campaigns_effect

§H解锁宣战理由：§!存在抹除

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

§HUnlocks Casus Belli:§! Existential Expulsion

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:974`

## tech_planetary_government

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government&lang=en)

### 中文

统一政府

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

Unified Government

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:977`

## tech_planetary_government_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民地建立完毕后，开拓者就变成了公民，殖民时期的灵活机关也就必须转变为能维持稳定的强力地方政府。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

Once a colony has been firmly established and pioneers turn into citizens, the need for a strong local government providing stability trumps the colonial need for flexibility.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:978`

## tech_planetary_government_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

统一实例化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Unified Instantiation

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:979`

## tech_planetary_government_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

只有面向我们的网络进行专门化的基础设施建设，我们的殖民地才能实现稳定的运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

Our colonies would never achieve stable operation without infrastructure dedicated to our networked self.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:980`

## tech_planetary_government_hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government_hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

统一簇丛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

Unified Plexus

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:981`

## tech_planetary_government_desc_hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_government_desc_hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

如果不能将集体意识进行稳定的投射，我们的殖民地会枯萎归零。我们不辞辛劳地现身是保证蜂群繁荣昌盛的关键。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

Our colonies would wither to naught without the stable projection of our collective self. Our effortless presence ensures a flourishing hive.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:982`

## tech_planetary_unification

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification&lang=en)

### 中文

社会统一

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

Societal Unification

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:983`

## tech_planetary_unification_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

上古时期的诸国家、部族曾经兵戎相见，然而现在一切都统一在帝国的旗帜之下。我们绝不会，也永远不会，灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

Ancient warring tribes, historical nations in conflict, now unified in empire. We must not, will not, crumble.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:984`

## tech_planetary_unification_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

统一数据标准

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

Uniform Data Standards

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:985`

## tech_planetary_unification_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

偏差不可接受。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

Deviance will not be tolerated.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:986`

## tech_planetary_unification_hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

演化收敛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

Evolutionary Convergence

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:987`

## tech_planetary_unification_desc_hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_desc_hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为了求生存而死命战斗的生态熔炉已经在我们的基因记忆中蚀刻了一条强健的道路。我们集所有曾来此地的存在于一统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

The ecological crucible in which we fought for survival has etched sturdy paths into our genetic memory. We are the peerless union of all that came before.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:988`

## tech_planetary_unification_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_planetary_unification_effect&lang=en)

### English

Grants a small amount of $r_unity$

## tech_adaptive_bureaucracy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_bureaucracy&lang=en)

### 中文

灵活机构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

Adaptive Bureaucracy

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:990`

## tech_adaptive_bureaucracy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_bureaucracy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于发展需要，新时代政府形式必须更加灵活有效，而不是碍手碍脚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

The progress we make calls for a new, nimbler form of administration that may aid rather than hinder us in our pursuits.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:991`

## tech_adaptive_bureaucracy_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_bureaucracy_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### English

## tech_adaptive_bureaucracy_desc_machine_intelligence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_bureaucracy_desc_machine_intelligence&lang=en)

### 中文

相比早期型号，正电子处理器造价低廉、生产快速，有利于引入额外领袖单位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:919`

### English

Positronic CPUs are both cheaper and faster to produce than previous models, allowing us to press additional leader units into service.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:993`

## tech_effective_bureaucracy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_effective_bureaucracy&lang=en)

高效吏治

用于处理和组织海量数据的先进算法可以大幅度优化我们在全帝国范围内的决策效率和信息共享能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### 中文

地区管理技术的进步使得我们可以对殖民地事务进行进一步的监控，而不会过度增加中央决策机构的负担。

### English

健全地方政府管理体制，因地制宜地满足各个殖民地的需要。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_galactic_administration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星际帝国行政事务繁多，问题复杂。着手妥善应对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

The administrative needs of a star empire are as numerous as they are complex. We will rise to the occasion.

## tech_living_state

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_living_state&lang=en)

### English

The Living State

## tech_living_state_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_living_state_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国生生不息。我们构筑成为帝国的躯体，但左膀无需知道右臂在做什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

The Empire lives, the Empire breathes. We may make up its body, but it is not for the left hand to know what the right hand is doing.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1005`

## tech_autonomous_agents

Autonomous Agents

半自主的单位是大局成功的关键。

### English

Semi-independent units have proved critical to the success of the greater whole.

## tech_collective_self

### English

## tech_collective_self_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_collective_self_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

团体内的求同存异是自然而然且不可避免的；最终，同性一定会比个性发挥更大的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

Finding aspects of the self within the group is natural and inevitable; eventually those shared aspects must be valued higher than individual identities.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1009`

## tech_embodied_dynamism

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_embodied_dynamism&lang=en)

### 中文

具现物力论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

Embodied Dynamism

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1010`

## tech_embodied_dynamism_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_embodied_dynamism_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有必要向我们的自主领袖单位提倡积极主动和抱负，但同时也要确保他们不会偏离核心智能的指示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

Initiative and some measure of ambition must be promoted in our autonomous leader units, while still making certain that they do not stray too far from the directives of the core intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1011`

## tech_ethical_equations

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ethical_equations&lang=en)

### 中文

道德方程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:938`

### English

Ethical Equations

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1012`

## tech_ethical_equations_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_ethical_equations_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

禁锢他人的同时释放自我是一项困难异常的思维杂技——现在我们得益于更为先进的装备终于可以达成这项壮举了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

Limiting the freedoms of others while preserving one's own may require unusual mental acrobatics - a feat we are now better-equipped to perform.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1013`

## tech_neural_implants

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_neural_implants&lang=en)

Neural Implants

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_neural_implants_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

所谓认知，不过是对感官信号的处理而已。它是可以被改变的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Cognition is little but the processing of sensory input. It can be altered.

## tech_global_production_strategy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_global_production_strategy&lang=en)

### English

Global Production Strategy

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1017`

## tech_global_production_strategy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_global_production_strategy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星球很大，而生产设施之间通常也相隔着很远的距离。我们需要一个协调策略，来确保每个人都在朝着相同的方向努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:944`

### English

Planets are big and production sites are often separated by vast distances. A coherent strategy for making certain that everyone is pulling in the same direction is often needed.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1018`

## tech_symbol_unity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_symbol_unity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们更多更深地了解这个星系的奥秘，重申我们的信仰并将我们的人民团结在同一面旗帜下的需求日益迫切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

As we explore more of the galaxy and its wonders, it becomes increasingly important for us to reaffirm our beliefs and to unify our people under one symbol.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1028`

## tech_visitor_center

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_visitor_center&lang=en)

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_visitor_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在了解到我们于太空中并不孤独后，我们应当做好准备，迎接这些新朋友的到来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

As we learn that we are not alone in space, we should begin preparations to welcome our new-found friends.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1030`

## tech_subdermal_stimulation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_subdermal_stimulation&lang=en)

### 中文

皮下刺激

### English

Subdermal Stimulation

## tech_subdermal_stimulation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_subdermal_stimulation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在感官改造工程与娱乐体验革命性交叉领域中，深层组织植入体无疑是先进的新发明。“我之所在，皮囊不将束之。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

Deep-tissue implants are the latest innovation in the revolutionary overlapping of sensory engineering and entertainment experiences. "Where we're going, we won't need skin to feel!"

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1032`

## tech_paradise_dome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_paradise_dome&lang=en)

### 中文

有了最先进的建筑学、景观学及全息影像技术，我们将建造一个真正的未来家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

With the latest advances in architecture, landscaping and holographic technology, we shall build the homes of the future.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1034`

Artificial Moral Codes

## tech_artificial_moral_codes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_artificial_moral_codes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

具体情境规定了到底什么是道德的。若拥有定义情景的能力，伦理道德不过手中黏土，可以随意摆弄。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

It is circumstance that dictates what is moral. Define the circumstances, and ethics is like clay in your hands.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1038`

## tech_synthetic_thought_patterns

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_synthetic_thought_patterns&lang=en)

跳出囹圄思考，唯一能够发现的是明白自己身处另一个更大的囹圄之内。

## edict_will_to_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_will_to_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果说权力是将自己的意愿强加于他人的能力，那么权力意志就是一种自证预言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:969`

### English

As power is simply the ability to impose one's will on others, the will to power is a self-fulfilling prophecy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1044`

## tech_unity_of_purpose

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_unity_of_purpose&lang=en)

### 中文

一个人的目标就是所有人的目标。唯有齐心协力，方可众志成城。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

The purpose of one is the purpose of all. Together, we are greater than the sum of our parts.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1047`

## tech_heritage_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_heritage_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对我们迄今为止走过的路的纪念。既是为了向过去的成就致敬，也为了激励我们勇敢走向未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1063`

## tech_heritage_site_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_heritage_site_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

重点测试管理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

Focus-Tested Management

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1064`

## tech_heritage_site_desc_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_heritage_site_desc_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

潜意识重点测试的发明大大改善了我们市场营销部门的效率。如能将这些方法应用到我们的智慧生命资源管理部门，将使我们成为企业管理方面的新标杆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

The invention of subconscious focus-testing has led to immense efficiency gains in our marketing and sales departments. By applying these methods to our sapient resource departments, we will achieve a bold new paradigm in corporate management.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1065`

## tech_hypercomms_forum_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hypercomms_forum_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

并不是所有关于超光速通讯频道的研究都是有用功，但是其中某些成果也许会找到将我们的人民团结在一起的新目的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Not all research into faster-than-light communication channels produces useful results, but some outcomes may find new purpose in bringing our people together.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1069`

## tech_hypercomms_forum_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hypercomms_forum_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

后相对论协作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

Post-Relativistic Synergizing

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1070`

## tech_hypercomms_forum_desc_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_hypercomms_forum_desc_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

只能以亚光速进行企业协作的时代早已成为过去。现在是超光速经济的新时代，而我们的企业文化也必须克服过时的相对论带来的限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

The days when corporate synergy could only be achieved at sublight speeds are long gone. This is a bold new age of faster-than-light economics, and our corporate culture must adapt to overcome its outmoded relativistic constraints.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1071`

## tech_autocurating_vault

## tech_autocurating_vault_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocurating_vault_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对种类迥异的数据集进行的持续而非同步的分析允许我们对于社会潮流中迄今为止难以预测的浪潮进行精准的数学建模。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:995`

### English

Continuous, asynchronous analysis of disparate data sets allows for the accurate modeling of hitherto-unpredictable fluctuations in social currents.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1073`

## tech_autocurating_vault_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocurating_vault_corporate&lang=en)

### English

## tech_autocurating_vault_desc_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_autocurating_vault_desc_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一座巨大的金库，公开展示我们公司的许多辉煌的成果，员工将从这些成功的接管经历中汲取灵感，并从被收购的公司不能很好地融入我们更宏大的商业战略的失败经历中获得警示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

A massive vault with public displays of our corporation's many glorious acquisitions will let employees draw inspiration from successful takeovers, and take warning of what happens when acquired companies fail to properly mesh into our broader business strategy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1075`

通过我们对物理以及力场操纵的理解，我们神圣的领域将为所有的个体所见所感。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_transcendent_faith_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

离开母星的摇篮为我们开启了理解信仰的新大门，而信仰的位置被摆在了更广阔的背景中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1005`

### English

## tech_alien_sanctuary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_alien_sanctuary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

先进归化中心的引进会给每个来到我们帝国的新成员提供特殊装备，以确保他们能适应新的社会，同时还可以保留他们天生的优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

The introduction of advanced naturalization centers will ensure that any newcomers to our empire are uniquely equipped to navigate their adoptive society, while preserving their native strengths.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1085`

## tech_amoeba_strike_craft_1

## tech_amoeba_strike_craft_1_desc

### 中文

通过调整太空变形虫的繁育流程，我们可以将其共生鞭毛改造为我们海军的生物舰载机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

By altering the breeding process of Space Amoebas, we can program their symbiotic flagella to act as organic strike craft for our navy.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1093`

### English

Swarm Spawning Pools

## tech_swarm_strike_craft_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_swarm_strike_craft_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从击败的索林原虫处收集样本能使我们克隆其基因并进行重编程，从而造出我们自己的战斗武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1014`

### English

Collecting specimens from defeated Prethoryn enables us to clone and genetically program a war-brood of our own.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1095`

## tech_scourge_missile_1

通过分析虫群使用的生物武器，我们可以造出同样可怕的自航炮弹用以传播杀伤。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_space_whale_weapon_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改进缇扬奇提取气态物质的能量的方法，就有可能造出可以从敌方舰船中汲取能量的武器。

### 中文

通过足够先进的数学仿真，也许有可能在一定程度上预测有机物种的经济浮动。我们可以借机操控他们的股市为我们牟利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1153`

### English

Through sufficiently advanced mathematical simulations, it may be possible to predict the fluctuating economies of organic society in some limited fashion. This would let us manipulate their stock markets to our advantage.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1246`

## tech_binary_motivators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_binary_motivators&lang=en)

### 中文

二进制内驱程序

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

Binary Motivators

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1247`

## tech_binary_motivators_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_binary_motivators_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些先进的内驱程序比早先版本量级更轻，能让机器人单位为子系统分配额外的空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1155`

### English

These advanced motivators are both smaller and lighter than previous versions, allowing robotic units to allocate additional space for subsystems.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1248`

## tech_nanite_assemblers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nanite_assemblers&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米装配机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1156`

### English

Nanite Assemblers

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1249`

## tech_nanite_assemblers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_nanite_assemblers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用程控的纳米机器人辅助建造新机器单位，使其拥有配备了更多子系统的更为先进的模板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

Using programmable nanites to assist in the construction of new robotic units will allow for more advanced models that are equipped with an even greater array of subsystems.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1250`

## tech_adaptive_combat_algorithms

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_combat_algorithms&lang=en)

### 中文

自适应作战算法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1158`

### English

Adaptive Combat Algorithms

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1251`

## tech_adaptive_combat_algorithms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_adaptive_combat_algorithms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有机生命体经常采用不合逻辑且不可预测的战术，这对我们来说很难有效应对。但采用自适应的战术以及新的、机动性更好的战体将能提供决定性的优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1159`

### English

Organics often fight using illogical and unpredictable tactics that can be difficult to effectively counter. Adaptive tactics and new, more maneuverable warforms should provide a decisive advantage.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1252`

## tech_biomechanics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_biomechanics&lang=en)

### 中文

生物力学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1160`

### English

Biomechanics

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1253`

## tech_biomechanics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_biomechanics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过数百万年坚持不懈的进化，有机生命有时也会进化出优雅简洁的解决方案，这可以应用到机械设计中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

Millions of years of organic evolution will sometimes, through sheer persistence, produce elegant solutions that can also be applied to machine designs.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1254`

## tech_singularity_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_singularity_core&lang=en)

### 中文

奇点核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

Singularity Core

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1255`

## tech_singularity_core_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_singularity_core_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在可控环境中研究捕获的黑洞有巨大的研究价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1163`

### English

A captive black hole that can be studied in a controlled environment would be an incredible research asset.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1256`

## tech_micro_replicators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_micro_replicators&lang=en)

### 中文

微型复制机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

Micro-Replicators

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1257`

## tech_micro_replicators_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_micro_replicators_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

配有显微设备的复制机能够让我们复制出带有更多复杂细节的无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1165`

### English

Microscopic tools that can assist our replication units in creating the more complex circuitry needed for new drones.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1258`

## tech_self_assembling_components

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_self_assembling_components&lang=en)

### 中文

自动组装部件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

Self-Assembling Components

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1259`

## tech_self_assembling_components_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_self_assembling_components_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

仿照有机物DNA而来，这些微型机械智能可以自动组成大多数新无人机需要的零件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/technology_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

Modelled on organic DNA, these microscopic machine intellects are capable of autonomously assembling most components needed for new drones.

Source: `localisation/english/technology_l_english.yml:1260`
